レポート

2026 年冬のボーナス見通し

～企業の好業績と人手不足を背景に堅調な増加が続く～

調査部 主席研究員 小林 真一郎 
研究員 小林 啓介

○ 2026 年冬の民間企業（調査産業計・事業所規模 5 人以上）のボーナスは、前年比＋5.0%と 6 年連続で増
加が見込まれる。好調な企業業績と堅調な雇用情勢が追い風となり、大幅な伸びになるだろう。 
○ 支給労働者割合は 87.3%（前年差＋0.8%ポイント）と 2 年ぶりに上昇するだろう。同割合はコロナ前の水準
を超えて回復しており、雇用者数の増加が続く中、ボーナスが支給される事業所で働く労働者の数は 4,577
万人（前年比＋1.9%）と、4 年連続で過去最多を更新する見込みである。 
○ 一人当たり支給額と支給労働者数の増加を受け、ボーナスの支給総額は 20.4 兆円（前年比＋7.0%）と 6 年
連続で増加しよう。ボーナスの支給総額の伸びは物価上昇率を大きく上回っており、個人消費の回復を下
支えすると期待される。 
○ 2026 年冬の国家公務員（管理職および非常勤を除く一般行政職）のボーナス（期末・勤勉手当）の平均支
給額は 77 万 9,100 円（前年比＋4.4%）と前年冬に続き、大きめの増加が見込まれる。景気回復に伴う民間
給与の増加が公務員にも反映される状況が続き、基本給、ボーナス支給月数とも引き上げられるとみられる。 
2026 年冬のボーナス見通し

(注 1)民間企業（調査産業計・事業所規模 5 人以上）は、賞与を支給する事業所で働く全常用労働者（当該事業所で賞与
の支給を受けていない労働者も含む）の平均 
(注 2)国家公務員は、管理職および非常勤を除く一般行政職の平均 
(注 3)支給労働者数は、賞与を支給する事業所で働く全常用労働者（当該事業所で賞与の支給を受けていない労働者も
含む）の数 
(注 4)支給総額は一人当たり支給額に支給労働者数を掛け合わせた値 
(出所)厚生労働省「毎月勤労統計」、内閣人事局資料などを基に当社予測

1. 2026 年夏のボーナス ～企業の好業績を背景に 5 年連続の増加

厚生労働省「毎月勤労統計調査」によると、民間企業（調査産業計・事業所規模 5 人以上）における 2026
年夏のボーナスの一人当たり支給額は、前年比＋5.3%と 5 年連続で増加した（図表 1）。前年（2025 年）の
伸びである同＋2.9%を大きく上回っており、2026 年は企業の好業績を背景にボーナスは堅調に増加した。 
業種別では、製造業（前年比＋5.8%）、非製造業（同＋5.3%）ともに増加した。内訳をみると、生活関連サ
ービス等（前年比＋25.7%）、建設業（同＋14.4%）、学術研究等（同＋12.9%）などが大幅に増加し、全体
の数字を押し上げた。一方で、複合サービス事業（同－2.1%）は減少した。 
ボーナス支給月数（所定内給与に対する比率）は 1.10 カ月分と前年から 0.02 カ月増加した。また、ボー
ナスが支給された労働者の割合 1は 85.1%（前年差＋0.5%ポイント）と小幅に上昇した。雇用者数が順調に
増加して過去最高水準を更新しており、ボーナスが支給された労働者数は前年比＋1.4%と増加した。夏の
ボーナスの支給総額（平均給与額×支給労働者数）は 19.9 兆円（前年比＋6.8%）と過去最高を記録した。

図表 1 夏のボーナス実績：平均支給額（前年比）と支給月数

（注 1）調査産業計、事業所規模 5 人以上  
（注 2）支給月数は所定内給与に対する支給割合 
（出所）厚生労働省「毎月勤労統計」

1 労働者の総数に対して、ボーナスが支給される事業所で働く労働者（当該事業所でボーナスの支給を受けていない労働者も含む）が占
める割合。

2. 2026 年冬のボーナス見通し

(1) 民間企業 ～ 企業の好業績と労働需給の逼迫を追い風に、6 年連続で増加する見込み

厚生労働省「毎月勤労統計調査」ベースで見た民間企業（調査産業計・事業所規模 5 人以上）の 2026 年冬
のボーナスの一人当たり平均支給額は 44 万 6,252 円（前年比＋5.0%）と 6 年連続での増加を予想する（図表
2）。企業規模を問わず、製造業、非製造業とも一人当たり平均支給額は増加すると予測する。 
今年の冬のボーナスが順調な増加を続けるとみられる背景に、企業業績の好調さと雇用情勢の堅調さがある。
企業の経常利益（全規模、金融保険業を除く全産業、季節調整値）は、2026 年 4～6 月期に過去最高を更新す
るなど好調に推移している。今後イラン情勢の展開次第では業績に下押し圧力がかかるリスクはあるが、冬のボ
ーナスへの影響は軽微だろう。 
また、人手不足は深刻化し、労働需給は逼迫している。日銀短観における雇用人員判断 DI は対面型サービ
ス業を中心に大幅な「不足」超が続き、企業の人手不足感が強まる中、完全失業率は 2％台半ばと低水準での推
移が続いている。

図表 2 冬のボーナス予測：平均支給額（前年比）と支給月数

（注 1）調査産業計、事業所規模 5 人以上  
（注 2）支給月数は所定内給与に対する支給割合 
（出所）厚生労働省「毎月勤労統計」

支給労働者割合は 2 年ぶりに上昇し、87.3%（前年差＋0.8%ポイント）となると見込まれる。コロナ前 2019 年
の水準を大きく超えた支給労働者割合に加え、雇用者数が堅調に増加していることから、ボーナスが支給される
労働者数は 4,577 万人（前年比＋1.9%）と、非製造業を中心に増加し、4 年連続で過去最多を更新する公算が
大きい（図表 3）。

図表 3 冬のボーナス予測：支給労働者数と支給労働者割合

（出所）厚生労働省「毎月勤労統計」

一人当たりボーナス支給額と支給労働者数の増加を反映した 2026 年冬のボーナスの支給総額（一人当たり
支給額×支給労働者数）は、20.4 兆円（前年比＋7.0%）と 6 年連続での増加が見込まれる（図表 4）。ボーナス
支給総額の伸びは物価上昇率を大きく上回っており、ボーナス支給総額の増加が個人消費の回復を下支えす
る要因になると期待される。

(2) 公務員 ～ 基本給、支給月数ともプラスに寄与し、ボーナスは 5 年連続で増加

内閣人事局の発表によると、国家公務員（管理職および非常勤を除く一般行政職）の 2026 年夏のボーナス
（期末・勤勉手当）は 73 万 8,500 円（前年比＋4.5%）と 4 年連続で増加した。昨年の人事院勧告に基づく給与
法改正でボーナスの基準となる基本給が前年比＋3.3%と増加したほか、ボーナス支給月数が昨夏から 0.025 カ
月引き上げられた。 
2026 年冬のボーナスの平均支給額は 77 万 9,100 円（前年比＋4.4%）と、5 年連続で増加すると予測する（図
表 8）。今年の人事院勧告に沿った給与法改正が行われることを前提に、平均給与額が増加（＋3.3%）するととも
に、ボーナス支給月数が昨冬から 0.025 カ月分（勤勉手当、期末手当がそれぞれ 0.0125 カ月分）引き上げられ
ると想定する。支給額は夏冬通じて過去最高を更新し、景気回復に伴う民間給与の増加が公務員にも反映され
る状況が続くとみられる。今後も、民間の賃金上昇傾向が続く中、公務員のボーナスも高めの伸びが続く見込み
である。 
図表 8 冬のボーナス予測（国家公務員）

## Document 107: 2027年・春闘賃上げ率の見通し ～賃上げ率は４年連続の５％台か～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2027年・春闘賃上げ率の見通し ～賃上げ率は４年連続の５％台か～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026年11月 17日

1/3 
2027年・春闘賃上げ率の見通し 
～賃上げ率は４年連続の５％台か～ 
 経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト  新家 義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

（要旨） 
○ 2027年の春闘賃上げ率を、厚生労働省ベースで 5.15％（2026年：5.18％）、連合ベースで
4.98％（同 5.01％）と予測する。2027年春闘でもほぼ前年並みの高い賃上げが続き、厚生労
働省ベースでは４年連続で５％台が実現するだろう。連合は 2027 年春闘の要求目安を前年
よりやや引き上げ、経団連も高い賃上げの継続に前向きな姿勢を維持している。ＵＡゼンセ
ンも前年並みの高い要求水準を示すなど、労使双方の賃上げ姿勢にトーンダウンはみられな
い。 
○ 構造的な人手不足が続くなか、人材の確保・定着に向けた賃上げの必要性は高い。企業収益
も総じて堅調であり、高水準の利益が賃上げ余力を支えている。2026年の物価上昇率が前年
から鈍化することは賃上げ率の下押し要因となる。一方、春闘交渉が本格化する 2027年初に
は物価上昇率が再び高まる可能性があり、実質賃金の安定的な改善を求める労使双方の問題
意識も強い。賃上げ率が一段と加速することまでは見込みにくいものの、高い賃上げを継続
する環境は維持されている。 
○ ４年連続で５％台の賃上げが実現すれば、日本銀行にとっても金融政策の正常化に向けて前
向きな材料となる。特に、労使双方が今後も２％程度の物価上昇が続くことを前提として賃
金を設定するようになっているかどうかが、２％の物価安定目標の定着を見極めるうえで重
要となる。

2027年春闘でも前年並みの高い賃上げ率を予想 
2027年の春闘賃上げ率を 5.15％と予測する（厚生労働省「民間主要企業春季賃上げ要求・妥結
状況」ベース）。2024年春闘では5.33％、2025年では 5.52％、2026年でも 5.18％と３年連続で
５％台の高い賃上げが実現したが、2027年もほぼ前年並みの高い賃上げが続き、４年連続で５％
台となるだろう。 
春闘においては、厚生労働省よりも公表時期が早い、連合による集計値が注目されることが多
い。この連合ベースの数字では、2027年の春闘賃上げ率を 4.98％と予測する（2026年春闘：
5.01％）。５％台に乗るかどうかは微妙なところだが、こちらも歴史的にみれば高い賃上げとなる

### Page 2

2/3 
だろう。

組合側、経営側ともトーンダウンはみられず  
組合側、経営側とも賃上げに前向きな姿勢を維持している。連合は 10月22日に公表した2027
年春闘基本構想において、定昇込みの賃上げ要求を「６％中心、５％以上」、ベースアップ等の賃
上げ分を「４％中心、３％以上」とし、前年の賃上げ「５％以上」、ベア「３％以上」よりも要求
姿勢をやや強めた。経営側も賃上げの継続に前向きだ。経団連の経営労働政策委員会報告原案で
は、高い賃上げの「社会全体への浸透」が掲げられていると報じられた。また、筒井経団連会長
は 11月９日の記者会見で、ベア実施の検討を賃金交渉のスタンダードとする方針を堅持する考え
を示した。また、主要産業別労働組合組織では、ＵＡゼンセンが定昇込み６％、ベア等４％とい
う前年と同等の高い要求方針素案を示している。中東情勢悪化によるコスト上昇という逆風があ
るなかでも、労使ともに賃上げ姿勢がトーンダウンしている様子は窺えない。もっとも、連合の
要求目安は強まったものの、ＵＡゼンセンの要求率は前年と同水準であり、現時点では賃上げ率
のさらなる加速を見込むほどの材料が揃っているわけではないことには注意が必要だ。

物価は前年から鈍化も、人手不足と好調な企業収益が押し上げ要因に 
高い賃上げを支える最大の要因は、構造的な人手不足である。生産年齢人口の減少が続くな
か、企業の人手不足感は依然として強く、人材の確保・定着は重要な経営課題となっている。特
に若年層を中心に採用競争が厳しくなっており、既存人材のつなぎ止めのためにも企業は継続的
な賃上げを行う必要がある。ここ数年の春闘では、こうした人手不足を背景に高い賃上げが実現
しており、企業の賃金設定行動にも変化が生じている。2027年春闘でも人手不足を背景とした賃
上げ圧力は強いと考えられる。 
好調な企業収益も、高い賃上げを支える材料になる。中東情勢悪化に伴うコスト上昇は企業収
益の下押し要因となっているものの、価格転嫁の進展やＡＩ関連需要の拡大などを背景に、企業
収益は総じて堅調に推移しており、水準も非常に高い。企業規模や業種によって差はあるもの
の、企業収益が全体として高水準にあり、賃上げ余力は十分に確保されていると見てよい。 
一方、物価については、2026年の物価上昇率が前年から鈍化することが賃上げ率の下押し要因
になるとみられる。春闘交渉では前年の暦年の物価上昇率が参照されることが多いが、2026年の
上昇率（ＣＰＩ総合）は前年比＋２％前後と、2025年の前年比＋3.2％から鈍化する見込みであ
り、物価高への対応という観点からの賃上げ圧力は弱まる可能性がある。 
もっとも、暦年でみれば物価上昇率は鈍化する一方、春闘交渉が本格化する 2027年初には再び
高まり、ＣＰＩは前年比＋３％台に達する可能性がある。この場合、実質賃金が再びマイナスに
転じる恐れもある。そもそも、過去数年にわたって実質賃金が減少してきたことで、家計には賃
上げの恩恵を十分に実感できないという不満が残っている。実質賃金の改善の遅れへの問題意識
は組合側、経営側にも共有されており、実質賃金を安定的に増加させることは 2027年春闘でも重
要な課題となるだろう。春闘交渉の時点で物価上昇率の再加速が意識されれば、春闘賃上げ率の

### Page 3

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
上振れ要因となる可能性もあることには注意したい。

２％「定着」判断の重要な材料に  
以上を踏まえ、2027年春闘では5.15％と、ほぼ前年並みの高い賃上げを予想する（厚生労働省
ベース）。賃上げ率が一段と加速することまでは見込みにくいものの、高い賃上げを継続する環境
は維持されていると考えられる。 
４年連続で５％台の賃上げが実現すれば、賃金上昇の持続性を重視する日本銀行にとっても前
向きな材料となるだろう。特に重要なのは、労使双方が今後も２％程度の物価上昇が続くことを
前提として賃金を設定するようになっているかどうかである。2026年の物価上昇率が前年から鈍
化するなかでも高い賃上げが維持されれば、こうした賃金設定が広がりつつあることを示す一つ
の材料となる。日銀は基調的な物価上昇率が２％の物価安定目標とおおむね整合的な水準に達し
たとの認識を示したが、２％の物価安定目標が「定着」したとまでは判断していない。2027年春
闘は、その定着を見極めるうえでも重要な判断材料となるだろう。

about EMC's share gain for
switch board CCL
Additional supply enquiries point to a potential revenue opportunity for EMC.
Our supply chain checks indicate that PCB makers supplying NVIDIA (covered by
Joseph Moore) have approached EMC (2383.TW, OW) for additional Copper Clad
Laminates (CCL) supply support for VR200 switch boards (32-layer PCBs). If these
enquiries translate into incremental orders, we estimate the resulting revenue

contribution would represent a single-digit percentage of EMC’s total revenue.
We expect EMC to retain a majority share of CCL supply for VR200 switch
boards. Our positive view reflects EMC’s technological capabilities and our
preference for market share leaders. While supplier diversification can strengthen
long-term supply security, we believe the ability to sustain yields and deliver
consistent product quality over time remains a key consideration in supplier

### Page 3

Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
12 November 2026 Citi Research
3
CPO: New growth opportunities from vertical integration — Hon Hai sees
significant long-term opportunities in both ASIC and vertical integration including
optical interconnect technologies. In optical networking, management believes the
transition from electrical to optical interconnects is structurally inevitable as AI
models expand and memory-bandwidth requirements increase. Hon Hai’s CPO

optical switching already starts mass volume production for its major customer,
and its micron-level precision manufacturing expertise in optical alignment,
connectors, and electrical-to-optical conversion could create additional value
through component integration and higher system-level content. Its extensive
engineering resources, ODM/JDM development capabilities, and vertically
integrated manufacturing platform should strengthen its competitive positioning.

Management expressed strong confidence in optical growth opportunities but did
not provide specific 2027 revenue guidance.

Source: Citi Research Estimates
Robust financials and earnings growth, maintain Buy — Thanks to strong
demand from major CSP clients and its continuous automation and vertical
integration development, Hon Hai’s capex increased 43% YTD. Nevertheless, its
EBITDA increased 50.3% YoY to NT$369.2bn. To improve capital efficiency, Hon

Hai plans to expand consignment arrangements, optimize inventory and
receivables management, and carefully evaluate investment payback periods.
Management reiterated its long-term operating margin floor of above 3%, with
further potential upside from scale economies, automation, product mix
improvements, and greater vertical integration.
Given Hon Hai’s solid result and better operational leverage, we raise our
2026/2027/2028 EPS by 4%/5%/5%. Considering its solid execution, stable

dividend yield and attractive valuation, we reiterate our Buy rating with TP NT$400
unchanged. Our TP implies 15x of our 2027 EPS projection.

Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)

9
Hon Hai Precision
Company description
Established in 1974, Hon Hai has become the largest EMS player in the world
in terms of sales. Through its vertical integration model or "eCMMS" (e-
enabled Components, Modules, Moves and Services), it provides
components, modules and system assembly products to many global OEMs
in the PC, communication and consumer sectors.
Investment strategy
We rate Hon Hai shares as Buy as we believe the company’s margins will

continue to improve, backed by a renewed focus on profitability, operational
efficiency and new businesses development. We think there’s more upside to
go from current level, as the underlying drivers are not just about product
cycle, but also about fundamental transformation and new business
opportunities.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$400 is based on 15x average 2026E-27E EPS, which is
at the higher end of the historical trading range. We believe this multiple is

justified by the company’s enhanced sales growth and profit margin outlook,
as well as new endeavors into AI server, EV, digital health and robotics. Hon
Hai has been able to generate positive earnings streams, and thus we believe
a P/E valuation methodology is appropriate.
Risks
Downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
include: 1) slower-than-expected iPhone demand; 2) intensified peer
competition; 3) weaker-than-expected execution on new business progress;

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

### Page 1

Merida (9914.TW)
3Q Briefing Takeaway – New Model Cycle to Drive 2027 Recovery; SBC 
Visibility Remains Limited 
CITI'S TAKE 
We expect Merida’s core business to recover in 2027E, supported by 
accelerating new model launches and largely normalized EU inventories. 
However, we expect the recovery in 2027E to be gradual with US demand 
remaining sluggish. Meanwhile, despite improving pre-tax profits, SBC’s 
profitability remains highly uncertain. We trim our TP to NT$67 factoring in 
a lowered NTM P/E of 11x and earnings cuts. Remain Neutral as we see 
limited near-term catalysts and prefer to await clearer evidence of 
sustainable SBC profitability and stronger core earnings momentum.
Outlook: More new model launches in 2027E to drive a sales comeback — Mgmt. 
attributed softer shipments this year to deliberate reductions in existing models 
ahead of more new product launches in 2027E. Both Merida’s own brand and its 
major OEM customer, SBC, have adopted this strategy, aiming to preserve pricing 
discipline and avoid excessive discounting. Mgmt. expects the inventory 
adjustment to conclude by year-end and most new models are scheduled to begin 
shipping from Jan-27, paving the way for a sales recovery in 2027E.  
EU leads the recovery; China stabilizes while the US remains sluggish — Merida’s 
own brand grew 14% y/y in 9M26 in EU, supported by solid demand for road and 
gravel bikes. The mgmt. believe inventory normalization is largely complete in EU. 
China’s own brand sales grew 1% y/y in 9M26, better than the original expectation 
of DD% decline, helped primarily by entry and mid end bikes. Mgmt. expects another 
single digit sales growth in China in 2027E, supported by resilient commuter 
demand. The recovery of US market, on the other hand, is lagging EU and China amid 
inflationary pressure and persisting macro uncertainties.   
Still low visibility on SBC profitability —The weak bottom-line of SBC was primarily 
due to a conservative effective tax rate assumptions rather than a deterioration of 
its fundamentals. SBC’s 9M26 pre-tax profits grew 42% y/y or +20% y/y excluding 
US tariff refunds, reflecting improving operating performance and cost discipline. 
While improving pre-tax profitability is encouraging, we remain cautious on SBC’s 
near-term earnings contribution until there is a greater clarity on its normalized 
effective tax rate and the sustainability of US profitability. (continued)

Earnings revisions and implications — We lower our earnings estimates by 20%
for 2026E and 6% for 2027E/2028E, mainly reflecting weaker-than-expected core
sales and profitability, alongside continued uncertainty surrounding SBC’s
profitability. We cut our TP to NT$67 based on 11x 4Q26-3Q27E P/E, down from
15.5x, factoring in weaker-than-expected core business and low visibility of SBC.
While we acknowledge the potential for a stronger sales recovery in 2027E as

Merida accelerates its new model launches, we believe the recovery will be gradual
rather than a meaningful acceleration in the underlying market demand.
Meanwhile, SBC’s uncertain net profitability and lingering US market weakness
remain the key overhang.  We would prefer to stay on the sidelines, awaiting clearer
evidence of sustainable SBC net profitability and stronger earnings momentum
from the core business. Reiterate Neutral.

### Page 5

Merida (9914.TW)
17 November 2026 Citi Research
5
Merida
Company description
Merida is the second-largest bike manufacturer in Taiwan. Founded in 1972,
Merida was initially involved in OEM manufacturing before it started
marketing bikes under its own Merida brand in 1986. Merida has expanded its
footprint globally through its Merida brand and three OEM brands:
Specialized (in which Merida has a 35% stake), Centurion (51% stake), and
Miyata (45%). Merida's own brand contributes around 30-40% of total

revenues. Merida has established a proprietary network of stores throughout
China, with 16 wholly owned subsidiaries and close to 2,000 retailers that sell
only Merida-branded bikes.
Investment strategy
We rate Merida shares as Neutral. Although bike inventories have normalised
in the major markets after aggressive clearance over the past 2 years, slower-
than-expected bike demand recovery continues to weigh on Merida's topline
momentum. The EU market appears to be where we should see better growth

for 2026E, while China market also turns better than expected from 2Q26; yet
with low visibility of SBC profit trend and limited positive catalysts in sight,
we expect the share price to be range bound.
Valuation
Our target price for Merida shares of NT$93 is based on a 4Q26-3Q27E P/E of
11x, a 50% discount to its historical average of 22x during pre-Covid years
(2014-2019), in view of soft order momentum as a result of slow recovery in
bike demand, a volatile margin trend, and low visibility of SBC.

Risks
Downside risks that could mean the Merida shares trade below our target
price include: 1) e-bike adoption slower than expected; 2) weaker restocking
demand; 3) lukewarm sell-through at SBC; and 4) a weaker macro.
Upside risks that could mean the Merida shares trade above our target price
include: 1) e-bike adoption faster than expected; 2) stronger restocking
demand; 3) stronger sell-through at SBC; and 4) a strong macro.
If you are visually impaired and would like to speak to a Citi representative regarding the details

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

新代(7750)：中國高階控制器需求持續強勁 
建議：評等買進

一、新代為台灣精密設備控制器
大廠 
 新代成立於 1995 年，為專注於精密設備控制器之大廠，以自有品牌 SYNTEC
行銷全球。主營產品涵蓋 CNC 數控系統、伺服產品與產業專機；集團亦積極佈
局新成長動能，旗下「聯達智能」與「正鉑雷射」分別專攻機器人/雲端智造
（IoT/MES）及工業減碳製程設備。公司具備兩大核心優勢：其一為精準的市場
定位，產品品質媲美德日大廠（如 Siemens、Fanuc），同時具備極佳的性價比，
成功填補高階進口與中低階國產間的市場缺口；其二為軟硬整合與在地服務，透
過橫跨歐美、亞太及中國 24 個分公司的全球據點，提供即時服務以協助機械廠
進行差異化升級。目前新代服務逾 2,000 家客戶，單一客戶營收占比均低於 5%，
客戶結構極具分散性與營運韌性，築起穩固的長期競爭護城河。

二、產品組合優化抵銷原物料成
本上漲，3Q26 毛利維持約 44%
水準 
 受惠中國工具機及金屬加工產業需求強勁、終端客戶持續升級高階控制器，以及
競爭對手關鍵零組件供應受限帶動轉單，新代 3Q26 營收達 78.05 億元，QoQ-
5.5%、YoY+157.7%，雖受傳統淡季影響較上季下滑，但仍創歷史單季次高。獲
利方面， 儘管記憶體價格上漲，受惠高階控制器銷售比重提升及產品組合優化，
3Q26 毛利率達 44.44%，季增 0.35 個百分點、年減 0.04 個百分點，維持公司
長期指引 45%±2%區間； 隨營收規模擴大及費用率改善，3Q26 營業利益達
21.06 億元，QoQ-11.9%、YoY+398.2%，營業利益率達 26.98%，年增 13.02
個百分點；稅後淨利達 16.80 億元，QoQ-11.4%、YoY+360.2%，單季 EPS 達
24.06 元，創歷史單季次高。1-3Q26 稅後淨利達 46.50 億元，YoY+221.7%，
EPS 達 66.59 元，反映高階產品需求成長、轉單效益及營運規模擴張持續挹注
獲利。

三、中國高階控制器滲透率持續
提升，海外布局與產能擴張挹注
2027 年成長動能 
 2026 年中國工具機產業維持成長動能，1–8 月金屬切削相關產業成長約 18–
19%，受惠製造業升級、五軸加工及車銑複合機等高階設備需求增加，帶動控制
器與伺服系統銷售。新代憑藉高階產品性能、較國際競品約 20%的價格優勢，
以及中國逾 2,000 家活躍客戶與在地直銷服務網絡，持續擴大市占率，2026 年
控制器出貨量預估 YoY+70–80%，並受惠日系競爭對手關鍵零組件供應受限，
取得大型工具機客戶轉單。儘管部分日系廠商供貨逐步恢復，公司透過提前備貨
及與記憶體原廠簽訂供應協議，預期關鍵零組件供應可維持至 2027 年下半年，
搭配產品組合優化及客戶黏著度提升，有望延續競爭優勢。海外布局方面，印度
市場受惠製造業在地化政策及工具機產業升級需求，帶動高階控制器國產替代
與供應商多元化商機。公司已與印度前十大工具機廠建立合作接洽，並取得兩筆
單筆超過 100 套的訂單，預期 2026 年印度營收 YoY+100%，惟目前營收貢獻

台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113)金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報 
仍低；中長期目標五年內印度市占率提升至 10%，並持續擴大東南亞市場布局。
產能方面，蘇州二廠預計於 2027 年農曆年前後完成搬遷，馬來西亞新廠預計
1Q27 完工，隨新產能陸續投入，預計 1H27 整體產能將擴充至目前約 2.6 倍，
另有機械手臂產能增加，為後續營收成長提供支撐。展望 2027 年，管理層預期
中國工具機產業成長約 10–15%，公司目標營收成長 25–30%，並持續推動新代
控制器、聯達機械手臂及正鉑智慧工廠整合方案，搭配海外市場拓展及 AI 技術
導入，帶動營收與獲利持續成長。

四、評等買進理由  綜合前述，展望 2027 年，新代受惠中國高階工具機需求成長、日系競爭對手轉
單效益延續，以及控制器市占率持續提升，營運成長動能可望延續。隨蘇州二廠
及馬來西亞新廠陸續投產，預計自 2Q27 起新增產能逐步開出，整體控制器產能
有望擴充至目前約 2.5 倍，搭配印度大型工具機客戶導入加速，以及多合一高階
控制器與智慧自動化產品持續推出，有助推升產品 ASP 及獲利能力，預估 2026
年稅 EPS 為 91.49，預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 為 118.49。至於評價面部分，過往
3 年 PER 平均落在 25x-35x，目前約 16x，處歷史區間下緣，中國市占率提升、
印度市場拓展、新產能貢獻及智慧製造業務成長等中長期動能，認為目前評價具
吸引力，故給予買進評等，目標價 2300 元(25x2026EPS)。

## Document 236: [000270] 기아(000270) 잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기기아(000270) 잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000270] 기아(000270) 잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기기아(000270) 잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung

### Page 1

기아 (000270)

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.15  
잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기

3Q26 Preview 
기아는 3Q26E 매출액 31.1조원(+8.2% YoY, -6.0% QoQ), 영업이익 2.2조원
(+53.1% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 7.2%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 
물량은 좋았으나, 아쉬운 믹스와 환율 
글로벌 도매 판매 84.7만대(+7.8% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 83.0만대
(+8.6%)를 기록했다. EV·HEV 확대에 따른 ASP 상승은 지속되고 있다. 전쟁으로 인
한 원자재 상승으로 3분기 재료비 부담은 전분기 대비 3,000억~4,000억원 증가할 
것으로 예상된다. EV 판매 비중은 2분기 13%에서 3분기 15%, 유럽은 35%에서 
43%로 확대되며 유럽 내 마진 희석은 불가피하다. 원화 강세에 따른 영업상 부담은 
판매보증충당부채 환입 등으로 상쇄돼 3분기 환율 영향은 제한적일 전망이다. 판매보
증충당부채 환입은 QoQ +5,000억원 수준으로 추정된다. 4분기부터 환율 효과는 부정
적일 것이며, 환율 10원 변동 시 연간 손익 민감도는 약 1,500억원 수준이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 165,000원으로 하향 
미국 조지아 공장의 텔루라이드 HEV(2.8만대)와 HMGMA의 스포티지 HEV(1.0만대) 
신규 생산으로 북미의 견조한 HEV 수요에 대응하며, 2027년 믹스 개선이 기대된다. 
유럽은 EV3·EV5로 점유율을 확대하는 가운데 유럽 내 할인율 하락과 EV 잔존가치 
회복으로 판매 여건이 개선되고 있다. 향후 IAA 규제에 따른 법인·플릿 혜택 제한은 
중국 업체의 원가 우위를 줄일 수 있을 전망이다. 
원화 약세 지속을 반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다.  
다만, 현재 주가는 12M Fwd P/E 기준 4.8배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 
수준, 저가 매수 기회라 판단된다. 현재 배당 수익률은 6% 수준으로, 상대적으로 높은 
배당 매력은 주가 하방을 지지할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr

## Document 247: [005380] 현대차(005380) 3Q26 Review_ 4분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up현대차(005380) 3Q26 Review_ 4분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up3Q26 Review_ 4분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005380] 현대차(005380) 3Q26 Review_ 4분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up현대차(005380) 3Q26 Review_ 4분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up3Q26 Review_ 4분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(005380) 
현대차 
2026.10.30

이재일 CFA 
6183 / lee.jae-il@eugenefn.com

3Q26 Review: 4 분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up  
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 600,000 원(유지) 
 현재주가 292,500 원(10/29)

 동사의 3분기 실적은 매출액 45.1조원(-3.4%yoy), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%yoy, OPM 5.9%), 지배이익 1.7조원(-24.6%yoy)을 기록하며 시장 
컨센서스를 상회. 도매 판매 감소, 원가 부담 증가 영향 있었으나 판매보증
충당금 축소와 원가 절감 활동을 통해 수익성 방어 
 실적 증감 요인: 영업이익 증감 요인은 물량 -1.11조원, 믹스/인센티브 -
0.57조원, 금융 -0.11조원, 환율 +1.28조원, 기타 +0.64조원 등. 원자재 
가격 상승으로 5천억원의 비용 증가 요인 있었으나 위기 대응 원가 절감 
활동으로 대부분 상쇄할 수 있었음. 전분기 대비 기말환율 급락함에 따라 
판매보증충당금 지난 해 1.26조원에서 340억원으로 약 1.23조원 감소. 판
보충 효과는 일회성에 그치겠지만 원가 절감 효과 컸던 점 긍정적  
 금융 부문: 금융부문 매출액은 전년비 2.8% 증가했지만 영업이익은 26% 
급감. 장기 금리 상승에 따른 이자 비용 증가와 기업금융 충당금 적립, 중고
차 관련 비용 확대가 수익성 악화의 주요 원인. 중고차 관련 비용은 EV 리
스 반납 차량의 매각 손실 확대에 기인한 것 
 4분기 전망: 동사는 국내 공장 정상화와 아반떼, 투싼, GV90, GV80 HEV 
등 주요 신차 효과를 통해 4분기 생산 확대와 주요 수출 시장 공급 회복이 
가능할 것으로 전망하였음. 원가 부담은 지속되나 물량 감소에 따른 영향에
서는 벗어날 수 있을 것. 원자재 가격 상승에 대응해 원가절감 프로그램 확
대와 신차 출시를 통한 인센티브 정상화를 통해 수익성 개선 지속할 계획 
 연간 가이던스 하향 조정: 글로벌 도매 판매 목표를 기존 416만대에서 
400만대로 하향 조정. 매출 성장률은 +1~2%, OPM 가이던스는 기존 
6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 하향 조정. 자동차 부문 투자 계획은 17.8조
원에서 16.0조원으로 축소. 핵심 R&D 투자는 유지하되 Capex 집행 시기 
및 우선 순위 조정할 계획 
 4분기 실적 반등 전망: 4분기 주요 신차(투싼, 아반떼, GV80 HEV, GV90) 
출시 본격화로 3분기까지의 부진을 만회할 수 있을 것으로 기대. 원가 부담 
은 비용 절감 활동을 통해 충분히 만회가 가능한 것으로 3분기 실적을 통
해 확인됨. 본격적인 주가 반등을 위해서는 판매 회복 이상의 주가 트리거 
필요할 것으로 보이나, 4분기 반전의 실마리가 나타나고 있다는 점에 주목. 
동사에 대한 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 60만원을 유지함

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율 
종목추천 및 업종추천 투자기간: 12 개월  (추천기준일 종가대비 추천종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함) 당사 투자의견 비율(%)

주가 추이

3Q26 매출액과 영업이익은 비현금성 비용 항목 개선으로 시장 기대치
를 상회했으나, 차량 판매 감소와 인플레이션 등으로 연간 가이던스는 
하향되었습니다. 올해 생산 차질에 따라 이연된 신차 판매를 ’27년에 
확대하며 매출/이익 회복을 가시화하는 부분에 주목 필요합니다. 
매출 성장은 금융부문이, 영업이익은 자동차부문이 기여 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 45.1조원(-3.5% 이하 YoY), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%)을 기록하며 시장 기대치를 상회했다. 파업으로 인한 생산 차질
로 3Q 판매량 92.1만대(-11.3%)에 그치며 자동차부문 매출은 큰 폭으
로 감소(33조원, -10.0%)했으나, 금융부문 매출이 취급 자산 증가로 큰 
폭으로 성장(+27.8%)했다. 반면, 영업이익은 금리 상승에 따른 금융부문 
영업비용 확대 영향과 기타부문 실적 부진 발생과 달리 자동차부문은 전
년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소와 판매보증 충당부채 전입
액 감소 등 우호적 환율효과로 큰 폭의 증가세(+24.8%) 기록했다. 
’26년 가이던스 하향, 구조적 반등 요인은 아직 
3Q 실적 발표에서 주목할 부분은 가이던스 하향과 현금흐름이다. 연간 
판매목표를 연초 415.8만대에서 400만대로 하향했고 영업이익률 목표
는 6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 0.8%p 하향했으며, ’26년 투자계획은 
17.8조원에서 16조원으로 낮췄다. 특히, 투자계획 하향은 차량 판매 매
출 감소 대비 인센티브, 원자재 가격 상승 등으로 현금흐름이 악화된 
부분에 기인한 것으로 판단된다. 실적발표에서 전사적 원가 최적화 활
동 추진을 강조했으나, 이는 좀 더 장기적 측면에서 효과가 가시화될 
것으로 판단되며, 인플레이션 및 원화절상 상황 고려 시 구조적 반등은 
단기적으로 쉽지 않을 수 있다. 
절치부심의 2027년, 목표주가 600.000원 유지 
그러나 매크로 상황을 제외하면  ’26년 하반기 생산 차질에 따른 신차 
판매효과가 ’27년으로 이연된 것이라는 점에 주목 필요하다.   ‘북미 HEV, 유럽 
BEV’ 전략 하에서 엘란트라, 투싼 풀세인지, GV80, G80 등 고수익 HEV 차종 
중심 미국 수요 대응을 통해 Mix 개선하는 모습이 확인되고, 생산이 본격화된 
아이오닉3 유럽 판매 확대 시 중국 로컬업체들의 공세를 방어하는 모습을 
확인하는 것이 가능할 것으로 판단한다. 노후차종 중심 운영에 따른 판매 둔화 
이외에 아직 근원적 판매 경쟁력 저하로는 판단하지 않는다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10 월 30 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 6

현대차 (005380) [한화리서치]

6 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

## Document 252: [005380] 현대차(005380) 저점을 받쳐줄 실적현대차(005380) 저점을 받쳐줄 실적저점을 받쳐줄 실적 - 기업

현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
292,500 
자동차업종

◼ 3Q26 비용절감/환율 효과로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현 
◼ 2027년 물량–환율 영향 상쇄되며 2H27 이익체력 개선 본격화 예상 
◼ 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등에 주목

투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 50만원 하향(-6%) 
현대차 TP 50만원(시총 102조원)은 12MF EPS 41,978원에 Target PER 12배
적용하여 산출. 제한적인 실적 기대 반영해 타겟 밸류 하향함에 따라 TP 조정  
우려 대비 양호한 3Q26 실적으로 주가 하방 부담 완화. 주가의 추세적 상승 
위해서는 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등 확인돼야 할 
것. 기저효과에 따른 물량 개선이 주가 저점 받쳐줄 것이나, 현재 밸류(2027년 
PER 9.1배vs 글로벌 피어 6.1배)에서 추세 상승 이끌긴 어려울 것이기 때문. 
이에, 자동차 본업 외 1) 한/미국 Physical AI 투자 계획 및 2) BD 공급망 구축 
/양산 가시화에 따른 주가 반등 기대

3Q26 매출 45조(YoY -3%, 이하 YoY), OP 2.7조(+6%), OPM 5.9% 시현 
3Q26 OP 추정(2.5조)/컨센(2.4조) 상회. 파업에 따른 생산/판매 차질 영향으로 
기대 제한적이었으나, 비용 절감/환율 영향으로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현. 
[자동차] 매출 33조(-10%), OP 2.1조(+25%), OPM 6.5%. 도매 판매 92.1만대
(-11%). 판매 부진과 원자재 부담에도 불구, 환효과(+1.3조)/비용절감(+0.5조)
로 실적 방어. 이외 YoY 변수별 영향은 물량(-1.1조)/믹스(-0.6조)/원자재(-
0.5조). [금융] 매출 9.2조(+28%), OP 4,710억(-18%), OPM 5.1%, [기타] 매출 
2.9조(+2%), OP 712억(-72%), OPM 2.5% 기록

2027년 매출 191조(YoY +2%, 이하 YoY), OP 12.7조(+15%), OPM 6.6% 전망 
기저효과 바탕으로 연결 도매 물량 YoY +5% 증가 예상하나, 원달러 환율 -
4% 하락(가정 환율 1,380원)하며 1H27 → 2H27로가며 이익체력 개선 예상

### Page 5

DAISHIN SECURITIES

5  
  현대차 3Q26 실적 컨퍼런스콜 QnA 
Q. 금융부문 환율 변동에 따른 4Q26 매출 및 손익 영향 
- 금융부문 환율 영향은 현대차 실적에 미치는 환율 영향과 동일한 방향으로 발생

Q. 3Q26 금융부문 수익성 악화 요인 
- 장기금리 상승에 따른 조달 및 이자비용 증가가 주요인. 자산/부채 만기 매칭에 따른 
금리 상승 영향 반영 
- EV 중심 중고차 가격지수 소폭 하락 및 차량 반환에 따른 매각손실, 금리 상승에 따
른 연체율 및 신용비용 상승 
- 차량 판매가격 인하 영향은 없으며, 3Q26 도매 판매 감소에 따른 금융 영업수익 영향 
불가피했음

Q. 향후 주주환원 정책에서 배당 및 자사주 매입 우선순위 
- 기존 밸류업 정책: 최소 DPS 1만원/TPR 35% 제시. 연간 배당 약 2.6조원 → TPR 
35% 충족 위한 잔여분 자사주 매입/소각 활용 
- 경영환경 변화 고려하여 기존 밸류업 프로그램 전반의 주주가치 제고 방안 재검토중 
- 새로운 정책 확정 및 주주 소통 전까지 기존 약속인 최소 DPS 1만원 유지할 계획 
- 시장의 배당 및 자사주 매입 간 균형 요구를 인지하고 있으며, 주주 의견 수렴 후 주
주가치 제고 계획 공유 예정

Q. 3Q26 영업이익 증감 요인 중 기타 +6,400억 발생 원인 
- 월별 컨틴전시 활동을 통한 비용 최적화 및 재료비 절감 지속 추진 
- 4Q26 원가 절감 효과는 3Q26 대비 확대 예상

Q. 3Q26 영업외손익 감소 원인 
- 3Q26 환율 급락으로 외화 매출채권 등 외화자산 관련 외화환산손실 및 외환거래손실 
발생 
- 매출채권 인식 시점과 회수 시점 간 환율 차이에 따른 외환차손 발생 
- 지분법 대상 기업들의 3Q26 실적 부진에 따른 지분법손익 감소도 일부 영향. 대부분 
외환 관련 손실에서 발생

Q. 중국 OEM 국내 진출 확대에 따른 대응 및 신차/가격 전략 
- 중국 OEM 및 테슬라의 국내 M/S 확대 대응중. 다만, 잦은 가격 조정은 중고차 잔존
가치 및 고객가치 훼손할 수 있어 지양 
- 현대캐피탈의 캡티브 금융 경쟁력 활용. 잔존가치 유예할부 등을 통해 월 납입금을 낮
춰 중국 OEM 대비 경쟁력 확보 
- 서비스 네트워크 기반으로 경쟁사 대비 경쟁력 확보 노력 
- 국내 신차: 4Q26 아반떼 HEV, 투싼, GV90, G80 HEV, 싼타페 FL(2026.12월), G90 
FL 등 출시 예정 
- 북미: 4Q26 투싼, GV80 HEV 출시 및 27년 초 GV90 출시 예상 
- 유럽: 아이오닉3 판매 개시, 4Q26 투싼/베이온 등 주요 모델 출시 예정 
- 중국: 아이오닉 V / 인도: 베이온, 아이오닉 P 등 출시 예정 
- 신차 출시 계획 연도별로 다수 예정. 가격은 원가/경쟁사/소비자 가격 수용성 등을 고
려해 결정

- Overweight(비중확대) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률 대비 초과 상승 예상 
- Neutral(중립) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률과 유사한 수준 예상 
- Underweight(비중축소) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률 대비 하회 예상

기업 투자의견 
- Buy(매수)  
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 10%p 이상 주가 상승 예상 
- Marketperform(시장수익률) 
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 -10%p~10%p 주가 변동 예상 
- Underperform(시장수익률 하회) 
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 10%p 이상 주가 하락 예상

## Document 289: [402340] SK스퀘어(402340) SK하이닉스 최대주주SK스퀘어(402340) SK하이닉스 최대주주SK하이닉스 최대주주 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[402340] SK스퀘어(402340) SK하이닉스 최대주주SK스퀘어(402340) SK하이닉스 최대주주SK하이닉스 최대주주 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

SK 스퀘어 (402340/KS) 
SK 하이닉스 최대주주 
SK 하이닉스 4 분기도 배당 주당 20,000 원 이상  
SK 하이닉스는 전일 주당 20,000 원(실적반영: 10,000 원, 키옥시아 특별배당: 
10,000원)의 3분기 배당을 발표하였다. 키옥시아 특별배당은 3분기 배당과 26년 회
계연도 결산배당으로 나누어 지급예정이다. 이에 SK 스퀘어는 2조 9,220억원(1분기
와 2분기 배당수입은 각 548억원)의 배당수입을 수취하게 된다. SK하이닉스는 26년 
전체 현금 배당 규모는 30조원 이상을 발표했는데, 3분기까지 지급하는 배당금은 14.6
조원이기 때문에 4분기에도 최소한 주당 20.000원 이상의 배당이 예상된다. 이 경우 
SK 스퀘어의 26 년 배당수입은 6 조원에 달할 것으로 예상한다. SK 스퀘어는 ‘26 년
~’28년 경상배당수입의 30% 이상을 주주에게 환원하는 주주환원정책을 보유하고 있
어 SK스퀘어의 현 시가총액 대비 27년 주주환원율은 최소 1.5% 이상 될 전망이다.

SK 하이닉스 자사주 취득·소각으로 높아질 지배력 
SK하이닉스는 20조원의 자사주 매입을 발표하였다. 이 중 10조원은 임직원 보상목적
이며, 10조원은 주주환원 목적이다. 26년 총 60조원 이상의 자사주 취득·소각 예정(8
월 결의분 40 조원 포함)으로 임직원 보상 목적의 자사주 매입은 제외되기 때문에 26
년 실적 확정 후 추가적으로 10조원 이상의 자사주 취득이 예상된다. 기 취득한 자사주 
40조원(3.3%)는 11월 5일 소각 예정으로 소각 이후 SK스퀘어의 SK하이닉스 지분
율은 20.5%(2 분기말 기준)에서 21.2%로 상승하게 된다. 이번 발표한 10 조원
(1.0%) 자사주 소각시 SK 스퀘어의 지분율은 21.4%이 되며, 추가로 10 조원을 추가 
소각한다면 21.7%까지 지분율이 상승하게 된다.

SK 하이닉스 대비 높은 베타 
SK스퀘어에 대한 투자의견 매수를 유지한다. SK스퀘어 NAV의 98%가량을 차지하
는 SK하이닉스의 현금배당 증가로 SK스퀘어의 주주환원 재원이 확대되고, SK하이닉
스의 자사주 취득·소각으로 지배력이 강화되기 때문이다. SK하이닉스는 전일 컨퍼런스 
콜을 통해 29년까지 D램과 HBM 물량이 사실상 모두 솔드아웃된 상황이라 밝히며 주
가 반등 가능성이 높은데, SK 스퀘어 베타(1.8)는 SK 하이닉스(1.7) 대비 높기 때문에 
SK 하이닉스 주가 반등에 따른 SK 스퀘어의 동반 주가 상승을 기대할 수 있다. 현재 
SK스퀘어의 NAV 대비 할인율은 46.5%이며, SK하이닉스가 자사주 60조원 소각시 
할인율은 49.4%까지 상승하게 된다.

2026-10-29

지주회사/통신서비스/ESG. 최관순 / ks1.choi@sks.co.kr / 3773-8812

## Document 302: Anthropic首日股价冲高回落，彭博称其IPO或标志AI行情终结

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic首日股价冲高回落，彭博称其IPO或标志AI行情终结.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

彭博经济学家 Anna Wong 认为， Anthropic ⾃ 9 ⽉以来每百万 token 的实际价格⼤幅下滑；
虽然 token ⽤量激增，但市场份额反⽽下降，说明其技术领先并没有转化为持久的定价权。
Anthropic ⾸⽇收盘较发⾏价上涨约 17% ，短暂提振市场情绪后，指数随即转跌，标普连跌
六⽇创 2024 年来最⻓连跌纪录。
摘要：
Anthropic 的⼤规模 IPO 看上去是对 AI 交易的信⼼投票，但彭博经济学家给出了相反的解读。
11 ⽉ 19 ⽇，据彭博经济学经济学家 Anna Wong 的分析，⾃ 9 ⽉以来，也就是 Anthropic 招股书所
载财务数据的截⽌期（ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇），其每百万 token 的实际价格已⼤幅下滑。
同期 token 使⽤量激增，但市场份额并未随之上升，甚⾄⼩幅⾛低。公司在让渡了相当⼀部分价
格溢价之后，并未换来更强的竞争地位。
简单来说，技术领先和旺盛的 AI 需求，并没有转化为持久的定价能⼒。对投资者⽽⾔，这意味
着 AI 交易赖以⽀撑的估值逻辑，正⾯临⼀次来⾃基本⾯的检验。

周四 Anthropic 以 ANT 为代码正式挂牌，上市⾸⽇， Anthropic 股价盘中最⾼较发⾏价上涨 34% ，
收盘报 97.17 美元，较 83 美元的发⾏价上涨约 17% 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

这⼀亮眼⾸秀短暂提振了市场对 AI 交易的情绪。标普 500 与纳斯达克 100 指数均⾼开，⻛险偏好
⼀度升温。然⽽，随着 Anthropic 股价⾃⾼点回落，整体指数随之转跌，标普连跌六⽇创 2024 年
来最⻓连跌纪录。
Anna Wong 认为，IPO 本⾝会把前沿 AI 的经济学搬进公开市场。从此定价、利润率和资本回报
将按季度接受审视。回过头看，这场 IPO 可能不像是 AI ⾏情新⼀轮上涨的起点，⽽更像是最后⼀
波燃料。
作为前沿模型领域的头部企业、 AI 热潮的标志性公司， Anthropic 暴露出的正是这轮交易核⼼处
的软肋：前沿智能的定价权正在被削弱。
定价权：护城河究竟由什么构成
持久的护城河，最终要落到攫取经济租⾦的能⼒上。 Anna Wong 的核⼼论点正建⽴在这⼀标准
之上：⼀家公司能否⻓期维持⾼于竞争⽔平的价格，决定了它的估值是否站得住。
以这⼀尺度衡量， Anthropic ⽬前呈现的图景并不理想。在招股书财务数据截⽌前的这段时间
⾥，其有效 token 价格陡降，价格溢价被⼤幅让渡，⽽竞争位置并未因此改善。技术上的领先优

势，没有在价格端兑现为议价能⼒。
Wong ⽤两张图表概括了整篇论证：其⼀，每百万 token 的实际价格近期明显下⾏；其⼆， token
使⽤份额并未抬升，反⽽略有下滑。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
价格与份额的这⼀背离，揭⽰的是 Anthropic 所处的市场结构 ——它更像是⼀个不具备持久定价
权的寡头。增⻓必须依靠降价来驱动⽤量，⽽⾮依靠产品差异化或客⼾黏性来⽀撑价格。 Wong
写道：
需要说明的是， Anthropic 此次巨额融资仍可能在短期内为 AI 交易再添⼀把⽕。资⾦⾯的⽀撑，
往往能在情绪层⾯延续⾏情。
若定价能⼒不及估值隐含⽔平，重估或加速
但 IPO 的另⼀⾯是透明度。上市之后，定价、利润率和资本回报将逐季披露并被逐季检视，此前
只存在于私募市场的定价能⼒问题，会被摆到公开市场的聚光灯下。估值所隐含的假设，也将
第⼀次接受连续性数据的对账。
Wong 提⽰的核⼼⻛险在于：如果 Anthropic 未来的业绩显⽰，这家公司的定价能⼒弱于其估值
所暗⽰的⽔平，那么随之⽽来的重估，有可能加速 AI 板块整体估值的回吐。
从更⼴泛的市场⻆度看，她近期还指出，根据美联储常⽤的利率分析模型（ Kim and Wright 模
型）测算，⽬前市场要求的⻛险补偿（利差）已经明显扩⼤。

与此同时，在融资条件趋紧的背景下，上⼀财报季中上市公司提及的  900 多项供应链瓶颈问题
仍有⼤量尚未缓解。这些⾼频信号共同构成了她对市场与信⽤⻛险的持续追踪。
Anthropic 上市提振昙花⼀现，指数开盘后迅速逆转
Anthropic 以 ANT 为代码正式挂牌，其旗下 Claude 聊天机器⼈此前⼴受关注。
此次 IPO 融资规模达 1073 亿美元，打破 SpaceX 此前保持的史上最⼤ IPO 纪录。上市⾸⽇，
Anthropic 股价盘中最⾼较发⾏价上涨 34% ，收盘报 97.17 美元，较 83 美元的发⾏价上涨约 17% 。
这⼀亮眼⾸秀短暂提振了市场对 AI 交易的情绪。标普 500 与纳斯达克 100 指数均⾼开，⻛险偏好
⼀度升温。然⽽，随着 Anthropic 股价⾃⾼点回落，整体指数随之转跌，开盘前的涨幅⼏乎瞬间
消失。
盈透证券⾸席策略师 Steve Sosnick 表⽰，标普 500 指数起初在 7300 点附近获得⽀撑，但 ANT 开
盘交易触发了新⼀轮抛售，该⽀撑位随后明显失守。

Walser 财富管理公司⾸席投资官 Rebecca Walser 对 IPO 持相对乐观态度，称此次上市 " 正是市场
提振整体情绪所需要的 " 。
但她同时指出，Anthropic 股价可能⾯临短期波动压⼒，尤其是前英伟达 CEO ⻩仁勋即将出席国
会听证，届时可能重燃外界对 AI ⾏业的担忧情绪。她说：
这是⼀⻔艰难的⽣意。
如果届时出现下跌，对于希望买⼊ Anthropic 的投资者⽽⾔，或许是⼀个不错的⼊场机会。

Topics  Regulatory reform in India's insurance sector

Over the past quarter century, the Indian government has pursued the liberalization of the country's insurance market, 
with the aim of bringing stability to people's living standards and strengthening financial intermediation functions. 
Recently, however, it has begun to reverse course, tightening rules in order to better protect consumers.

■ India's insurance market has grown rapidly with deregulation 
The Indian government has long pursued 
the development of the country's insurance 
market, with the aim of bringing stability to 
people's living standards and strengthening 
financial intermediation functions. This 
stance can be attributed to two background 
factors:  
The first is that India's social security 
system remains underdeveloped. As a result, 
enrollment in health and life insurance can 
provide a vital safety net for mitigating the 
risk of sharp declines in household income 
due to unexpected illnesses or accidents.  
The second is that the sustainable 
development of the Indian economy requires 
the accumulation of long-term and stable 
domestic capital, separate from short-term 
bank deposits, in order to finance the 
development of infrastructure and the 
nurturing of industry. The hope has been that 
the enormous sums of money collected and 
managed by insurance companies can serve 
as a reliable source for this long-term 
financing.  
These factors have led the Indian 
government to walk a path of gradual 
deregulation over the past quarter century. 
The process began with the liberalization of 
market entry by private and foreign 
companies in 2000, which was followed by 
a lifting of the ban on "bancassurance" (the 
sale of insurance through bank branches) in 
2002, the raising of the ceiling on foreign 
ownership, and other reforms.  
This series of deregulatory measures 
spurred the entry of numerous private and foreign 
firms, causing market competition to intensify. Several 
Japanese insurers, including Nippon Life and the Tokio 
Marine Group, also entered the market, helping to drive 
investment in India.  
Besides deregulation related to market entry and 
sales, other factors also contributed to the rapid growth 
of India's insurance market, namely the expansion of 
the middle class, improvements in household financial 
literacy, and the digitalization of insurance services. 
Premium income grew at a pace exceeding economic 
growth, and in recent years insurance companies' assets 
under management have reached a level equivalent to 
Year Event
Establishment of the IRDAI (Insurance Regulatory
and Development Authority of India)
Acceptance of market entry applications from
domestic private firms and foreign entities
(foreign equity cap set at 26%)

through banks or bank-owned distribution
channels)

3 
incentives to sell products and inappropriate sales practices. This should shore up the credibility of the 
insurance market and the foundation for its sustainable development. 
On the minus side, there are worries about side effects arising from the transition. As rules on selling 
become stricter, insurance companies and the financial institutions acting as insurance agents will inevitably 
see some temporary moderation in the pace of their earnings growth. Moreover, given that insurance 
companies and financial institutions are major investors in India's financial markets, tighter regulation is 
likely to exert downward pressure on equity and bond prices. 
These changes in the insurance market provide an important perspective for assessing the future 
trajectory of the Indian economy. They illustrate that rapid market expansion driven by liberalization can 
create a variety of problems. And this dynamic is not limited to the insurance sector, as it can also occur 
across many other industries. At the same time, the redesign and recalibration of regulations in response to 
such developments can provide a foundation for a new stage of growth. The new insurance regulations 
could constitute an early example of this phenomenon, so will be closely watched to see what changes they 
bring in the years ahead.  
  (Shotaro Kumagai)

ASIA MONTHLY REPORT  November 2026, No.308

The Japan Research Institute, Limited

Topics  Talent travel curbs may weigh on China’s growth

In September, the Chinese government introduced new exit-entry rules. Indicative of an effort to retain talent in order 
to prevent technology leakage, the rules could narrow opportunities for China to absorb overseas knowledge and weigh 
on its economic growth over the medium to long term.

■ Scope of overseas travel restrictions expanded 
On September 15, the Chinese 
government brought in new rules 
specifying conditions and screening 
procedures for entry and exit. The new 
rules explicitly state that the Ministry of 
Commerce or other departments may 
prohibit Chinese citizens from leaving the 
country if they violate export-control or 
technology import/export regulations and 
are deemed likely to threaten national 
industrial or technological security.  
Under China's Exit and Entry 
Administration Law, which came into 
effect in 2013, defendants and suspects in 
criminal cases, as well as persons involved 
in unsettled civil cases whom courts have 
prohibited from leaving the country, had 
already been subject to exit restrictions. In 
addition, Chinese citizens could be denied 
permission to leave the country if the 
competent departments determined that 
their departure might endanger national 
security or interests. Aside from formal exit 
bans, personnel who handle state secrets 
and officials in the public sector have long 
been subject to controls such as centralized 
passport management and the need to 
obtain approval in advance of overseas travel. What the new rules effectively do is make it clear that exit 
can also be restricted when there are issues from the standpoint of economic security. 
Against this backdrop, it was reported at the end of September that overseas travel restrictions already 
imposed on key personnel in the AI and semiconductor sectors had been expanded to include their spouses, 
children, and other family members. According to these reports, these key personnel include prominent AI 
startup founders, senior executives at major private enterprises, and core R&D talent. Although the measure 
does not constitute a blanket travel ban, affected individuals and their families are reportedly required to 
obtain government approval for exit even in the case of short private trips or business travel. 
This regulatory tightening is thought to be related to the technological rivalry between the U.S. and China. 
As competition surrounding critical technologies such as generative AI intensifies, the Chinese government 
appears to view not only technologies themselves, but also the researchers and engineers who create them, 
as strategic assets. By widening travel curbs to encompass family members, the authorities are likely 
seeking to prevent overseas emigration by individuals or the relocation of R&D operations abroad. A 
noteworthy aspect of the recent reports is that the controls apparently now extend beyond the public sector 
to key personnel at private enterprises, as well as their families. Restrictions like these on talent mobility 
could undermine the mechanisms of technology absorption and productivity improvement that have 
hitherto facilitated China's growth.

### Page 6

ASIA MONTHLY REPORT  November 2026, No.308

The Japan Research Institute, Limited

5 
■ China's productivity gains have been supported by market opening and technology 
absorption 
Productivity exerts a substantial influence on 
economic growth. Total factor productivity (TFP), an 
indicator of an economy's overall production 
efficiency, provides an estimate of efficiency in 
production that cannot be explained solely in terms of 
inputs of capital and labor, and reflects factors such as 
levels of technology and efficiency of resource 
allocation. 
China's TFP has been rising for many years. In 
addition to the transition to a market economy and the 
growth of private enterprises since the country adopted 
the policy of reform and opening up, Chinese 
productivity has benefitted from the absorption of 
foreign technology and managerial know‑how through 
trade and direct investment. A labor shift from 
agriculture to manufacturing and other sectors, along 
with competition among firms and the weeding out of 
weaker players, has also helped deliver productivity 
gains across the economy.  
Nevertheless, China's TFP remains low relative to 
other major economies. According to estimates from 
Penn World Table, China's TFP in 2023 was less than 
half that of the U.S., and it was also below the levels not 
only of Germany and other major European economies 
and of South Korea and Japan, but of Thailand and 
Malaysia, too. Factors behind this include inefficient 
resource allocation and the slow diffusion of advanced 
technologies and management practices. Preferential 
financial and institutional treatment for state‑owned 
enterprises, regional protectionism, and mechanisms 
that impede market entry and exit have distorted the 
allocation of resources, preventing capital and labor 
from being fully utilized by more productive firms and sectors. Moreover, although China has achieved 
strong competitiveness in certain advanced industries, such as electric vehicles and semiconductors, the 
leading-edge technologies and management practices developed in these sectors have not sufficiently 
diffused to the service sector and small and medium‑sized enterprises, which form the foundation of the 
economy, and this has constrained overall productivity growth.  
■ Moves to contain technology could hinder growth over the medium to long term 
Efforts to retain talent in order to prevent technology leakage could further delay China's productivity 
catch‑up with advanced economies. While restrictions on overseas travel by elite professionals may help 
with the retention of domestic R&D talent and protection of current technological advantages, they could 
also reduce opportunities to absorb overseas knowledge and to jointly develop new technologies with 
foreign partners. The transfer of technology and know‑how does not occur solely through the sharing of 
"explicit knowledge," in the form of academic papers, technical blueprints, and so on. "Tacit knowledge," 
which is transmitted through face‑to‑face discussion, collaborative work, and the acquisition of experience 
in production settings, is also crucial. If travel controls delay or prevent participation in international 
conferences, joint research projects, and technology exchanges with overseas companies, opportunities to 
absorb such knowledge may be lost, slowing technological innovation and improvements in production 
processes. Furthermore, if obtaining travel approval becomes more time‑consuming or less predictable, the 
overseas assignment or international deployment of technical personnel could also be obstructed. 
The new rules also cover the entry of foreign nationals. If uncertainty surrounding the administration of 
entry and exit controls increases, it could create obstacles to attracting and retaining highly skilled talent 
from abroad. At a time when inefficient resource allocation and the slow diffusion of advanced knowledge 
are already weighing on productivity growth in China, curbs on people-to- people exchange could further

impede both the absorption and creation of knowledge, as well as its practical application. Although the 
magnitude of the impact will depend on the scope of application and actual implementation on the ground, 
restrictions on talent exchanges and overseas travel that reflect excessive emphasis on technology 
protection could slow China's TFP growth over the medium to long term and act as a headwind to the 
Chinese economy by lowering its potential growth rate. 
(Hiromu Komiya)

### Page 4

Monthly Economics
Sector Analysis
Personal Consumption Expenditures (PCE)
• We did not materially alter our real PCE forecast, and expect consumer spending growth to
continue.
Consumer spending has held up remarkably well this year despite higher gasoline costs weighing on
household purchasing power. While we do not expect the recent above-trend near-4% annualized
pace of real consumption growth to be sustained, we see consumers continuing to spend at a healthy

clip into next year. A firmer income backdrop revealed by recent revisions, continued support from
household wealth and an expected recovery in purchasing power as inflation slows should keep
consumption on solid footing. We expect real consumption growth to remain north of 2% in the final
quarter of the year and roughly match this year’s average pace in 2027.
Investment: Equipment, Intellectual Property Products, and Inventories
• A stronger-than-expected capex outturn in Q3 has revealed a slightly firmer trend headed into

next year.
Business investment continues to be driven by the high-tech buildout, with real equipment spending
stronger than expected in Q3 and the latest data suggest a firmer trend. We have not materially
changed our outlook following the Democratic sweep in the midterms. Our forecast already assumes
some moderation in high-tech investment next year and a smaller contribution to GDP growth as the
buildout matures. Beyond high-tech, we expect only a modest broadening in capex spending over the

coming year.
Source: U.S. Department of Commerce and Wells Fargo Economics
Source: U.S. Department of Commerce and Wells Fargo Economics
Investment: Residential
• We have not made major changes to our outlook for residential investment. High mortgage rates
and challenging affordability conditions look set to continue pressuring home sales and new single-
family construction.
Not much has changed in the way of residential investment. Mortgage rates have come in somewhat,

but are still elevated above 7.0%. Building permits are stuck in a low gear, with single-family projects
continuing to moderate against a backdrop of weak buyer demand and elevated inventories.
Multifamily permits have picked up modestly, reflecting renewed strength in apartment demand.
Modestly lower mortgage rates and stabilizing apartment market conditions should help put a floor
beneath residential investment and bring about modest growth over the course of next year.

Investment: Nonresidential Structures
• Structures investment is likely to maintain healthy momentum amid robust data center, power, and
related nonresidential construction.
4 | Economics

### Page 5

Economic Outlook: November 2026 Economics
Structures investment accelerated notably in Q3. The bulk of this momentum can be traced to data
centers and power, however, activity also appears to be broadening out. As examples, private spending
on communication and transportation infrastructure have perked up and manufacturing construction
appears to be stabilizing on increased semiconductor investment. The downturn in commercial
construction has also softened as data centers shore up demand for warehousing. Although new

regulations may soften the pace of data center starts, there are more than enough projects already in
the pipeline to support healthy structures investment through next year.

Net Exports & Trade
• Net exports should remain a fairly persistent drag on U.S. real GDP growth amid continued
imports.
A surge in imports, particularly of high-tech goods like computers, caused net exports to subtract

more than 3 percentage points from Q3 GDP growth. We expect trade to remain a drag in Q4, though
with little data in hand, that largely reflects where trade flows ended last quarter and an assumption
of another sizable gain in imports. Looking ahead, continued strength in high-tech investment should
keep import growth elevated and the trade deficit wide, leaving net exports a persistent drag on U.S.
growth. Exports remain more volatile, but imports are increasingly the bigger driver of the trade story.

Labor Market
• Labor demand has improved modestly in recent months. We now expect payroll growth to average
65K per month in 2027, and the unemployment rate to hold steady at 4.1% over the next four
quarters.
The October jobs report reinforced our view that the labor market remains solid. The pickup in job
growth this year aligns with purchasing manager surveys that point to expanding employment across
the goods and services sectors. At the same time, the historically low trends in initial jobless claims and

the layoff rate suggest employers broadly remain reluctant to shed workers. Structurally, firms appear
focused on retaining existing staff and investing heavily in AI adoption while remaining selective about
adding to head count—a dynamic that should keep the low-hire, low-fire environment largely intact.
We expect payroll growth to average around 65K per month in 2027, roughly in line with our estimate
of breakeven job growth. That should keep the unemployment rate steady at 4.1% through next year

and labor market conditions generally consistent with full employment.
Inflation
• We look for the core PCE deflator to rise 3.3% on a Q4-over-Q4 basis in 2026 before slowing to
2.6% in 2027.
The October inflation report reinforced the persistence of price pressures, with upside surprises
emerging across a range of categories. Although gasoline and diesel prices have receded in recent
Economics | 5

### Page 6

Monthly Economics
weeks, earlier increases in transportation costs are still working their way through supply chains. Food
inflation picked up in October and looks poised to strengthen further. The outlook for energy prices
remains highly uncertain and represents the largest swing factor for inflation, in our view. Within the
core, we continue to expect goods inflation to moderate in 2027 as tariff-related pressures fade
further and demand tied to the AI buildout cools. Services inflation, however, is likely to prove stickier.

Health care services prices have been running hot and should continue to face upward pressure from
the aging population, and primary shelter inflation looks to be stabilizing. Taken together, we expect
inflation to remain above the Fed's 2% target through next year and look for the core PCE deflator to
end 2027 at 2.6% on a Q4-over-Q4 basis.

Fiscal Policy
• The lame duck session of Congress probably will not yield a budget reconciliation bill or a debt

ceiling resolution. The December 11 government funding deadline probably will be punted to Q1/
early Q2 via another continuing resolution.
• We project a federal budget deficit of $2.0 trillion for FY 2027, similar to FY 2026 in dollar terms
but a bit smaller as a share of GDP (~6%).
Assuming that Congress does not increase or suspend the debt ceiling in the next six weeks, the
incoming Democratic Congress will need to negotiate a resolution with President Trump in 2027.

Right now, it looks like Treasury will hit the debt ceiling around early March. At that point, Treasury can
lean on extraordinary measures and its cash on hand to last for about 7-8 months, putting the debt
ceiling "X date" somewhere around early Q4. We suspect the debt ceiling/annual budget negotiations
will include a lift to defense and nondefense discretionary government spending for FY 2027/2028,
but we doubt the bump will be enough to meaningfully move our economic growth or deficit forecasts.

For now, our working assumption is that divided government will not produce any major new fiscal
expansions/contractions over the next two years, leaving the budget deficit picture as status quo until
after the 2028 election.
Monetary Policy & Interest Rates
• Our baseline forecast is for another 25 bp rate hike to the fed funds rate in December, followed by
an extended hold from the FOMC through 2027. The risks are tilted toward 1-2 more hikes early

next year.
• Our forecast for the 10-year Treasury yield at year-end 2027 is 4.85%.
The recent inflation data seem to make a hike in December a lock so long as financial conditions don't
completely implode in the coming weeks. The core CPI came in about 8 bps above consensus at 0.3%
for October, and a 0.4% on core PCE is plausible, depending on how the PPI data shake out. This should
put the FOMC's hawks back in the driver's seat after the October skip.

6 | Economics

### Page 7

Economic Outlook: November 2026 Economics
Past December, the outlook will be highly dependent on future inflation data. Our expectation is that
core inflation will cool gradually in 2027, and we still do not see clear signs of demand-side inflation in
the data. But hiking cycles have an inertial component to them, and the recent inflation data reset the
clock to zero on the Fed seeing several good inflation prints in a row. This creates some modest upside

risk to our base case for the fed funds rate next year.

International Developments
• Global growth has proven more resilient, with our 2026 growth forecast revised higher and a
modest improvement in the underlying 2027 outlook.
• Our 2026 inflation forecast has moved slightly lower, while our 2027 forecast has moved higher,
with inflationary risks likely to persist into 2027.
• The monetary policy bias remains toward extended tightening across advanced economies.

Emerging market policy paths remain differentiated, but have also shifted in a more hawkish
direction, with some central banks bringing forward rate hikes and others having less room to ease
in 2027.
The balance of risks to global inflation remains tilted to the upside. Elevated geopolitical uncertainty,
resilient activity and persistent energy and supply-chain pressures increase the risk that inflation
remains higher for longer. Although the direct effects of supply shocks may prove temporary, central

banks are increasingly focused on delayed pass-through, persistent services inflation and potential
second-round effects on wages, prices and inflation expectations. Higher government bond yields are
meanwhile keeping financial conditions tight. The result is a shift toward more extended tightening,
with several rate hikes moving further into the forecast horizon and less room for easing in 2027.
Across advanced economies, the tightening cycle is increasingly extending into 2027. Our European

Central Bank outlook is unchanged, with a 25 bps hike in December still our base case, although
tighter financial conditions raise the risk of a later move. In the U.K., our November Bank of England
hike was realized, and we now expect another 25 bps hike in Q1-2027, reflecting renewed inflation
pressures associated with the Ofgem price-cap reset.
Elsewhere, the policy outlook has turned more hawkish in Canada. Having previously flagged upside
risks to our year-end call, we now expect the Bank of Canada to hike at consecutive meetings, bringing

the policy rate to 3.00% by Q1-2027. In Australia, the Reserve Bank of Australia raised the Cash Rate
again in November. After five hikes, we think policymakers have room to assess how tighter policy
feeds through the economy, although risks remain skewed toward another increase if inflation stays
elevated or activity proves stronger than expected. Japan is unchanged: we continue to expect the
Bank of Japan to hike in December and again in 2027, bringing the terminal rate to 1.75%.

Emerging market policy paths remain differentiated but have also shifted more hawkishly. In India,
resilient growth and rising inflation have led us to bring forward our second Reserve Bank of India
Economics | 7

### Page 8

Monthly Economics
hike from Q1-2027 to December 2026. Brazil is moving toward higher-for-longer policy, with
persistent inflation raising the bar for additional easing; we now expect cuts to resume in Q2 rather
than Q1-2027 and see less easing overall next year. In Mexico, Banxico's cut to 6.25% was more easing
than we expected, and with inflation risks still tilted to the upside, we now expect rates to remain on
hold. China remains the exception: continued targeted policy support and resilient exports should

support growth, prompting most upward revisions to our 2026 and 2027 growth forecasts, while
greater scope for easing likely emerges in 2027.

on the table, and improving domestic risk sentiment is creating
more room for manoeuvre. Nevertheless, we only see the next
cuts coming in late 2027 as inﬂation pressures rebuild

Peter Virovacz and Zoltán Homolya

### Page 2

The National Bank of Hungary kept the base rate unchanged at
5.50% on 17 November, in line with widespread expectations. This
marked the third consecutive meeting with rates being kept on
hold. While the latest communication revealed somewhat more
room for monetary easing, we think the window of opportunity will
remain narrow.
Looking ahead, the exceptionally favourable domestic inﬂation
backdrop is likely to fade relatively quickly. We see inﬂation

accelerating to 3.0% by December 2026, with repricing at the start
of next year temporarily pushing price pressures closer to 3.5% in
the ﬁrst quarter of 2027. Inﬂation could then approach 4% during
the summer, largely due to base eﬀects. In our view, this
increasingly challenging inﬂation proﬁle means that any near-term
opportunity for easing may prove short-lived.
In the meantime, however, we see the latest dovish shift in
communication potentially testing the waters and gauging market

sensitivity to eventual easing, particularly in a global environment
where hawkishness still dominates. The key litmus test will be the
forint: a muted FX reaction to the dovish rhetoric would give the
central bank greater conﬁdence that some easing could be
delivered without jeopardising market stability. This means that a
possible one-and-done December rate cut has become a clear
dovish risk to our baseline.
However, we believe that any near-term window for easing would

be relatively narrow. As 2027 progresses, the main constraint on
monetary policy is likely to shift gradually from external risks
towards the domestic inﬂation outlook. With inﬂation expected to
move closer to 4% during the summer, we see little room for a
sustained easing cycle over the next six to nine months. Any near-
term easing would therefore be more likely to represent an
opportunistic move than the beginning of a conventional cutting
cycle.

A more durable easing window could open later in 2027, once
inﬂation starts to decelerate more convincingly after the summer. If
this coincides with a benign external environment and a further

### Page 3

decline in the risk premium on Hungarian assets, we see room for
one or two rate cuts in the fourth quarter of 2027. Our baseline
therefore sees the base rate reaching 5.00% by end-2027, followed
by a further reduction to 4.50% by end-2028.
Our three takeaways from the presser

### Page 4

Review Policy rate NBH National Bank of Hungary
Monetary policy Hungary
1. A cut was already on the table. The biggest dovish surprise
came during the press conference. The Monetary Council
considered two options: keeping rates unchanged or cutting
them, while the decision itself was not unanimous. This
suggests that the bar for easing may already be lower than
previously thought. The December Inﬂation Report will now be
particularly important, with Governor Mihály Varga explicitly

stating that favourable developments in Hungary's risk
assessment and inﬂation outlook, together with reduced
external uncertainty, could increase its room for manoeuvre.
2. Hungary's risk story has improved. Perhaps the most notable
change in the narrative is the assessment of Hungary's risk
premium. Varga highlighted the favourable market reception of
the government's ﬁscal plans and its commitment to euro
adoption as factors supporting Hungary's risk assessment and

increasing room for monetary policy. The forint has also
remained stable and is stronger than a year ago, helping to
contain inﬂationary pressures. In other words, domestic
ﬁnancial market developments are becoming less of a
constraint on monetary policy than the Monetary Council
previously considered them to be.
3. The external environment still demands caution. The greater
room for manoeuvre does not mean that the NBH is ready to
embark on an easing cycle. Energy price volatility, elevated

global yields and above-target inﬂation in major economies
continue to warrant caution. At home, inﬂation is also expected
to accelerate from its current 2% level due to fuel prices, tax
changes and other policy measures. This leaves the Monetary
Council in a familiar data-dependent position: its room for
manoeuvre has increased, but whether it can actually use that
room will depend on both the external environment and the
December inﬂation outlook.

Join ING’s Dmitry Dolgin, James Wilson and Egor Fedorov on

Caucasus and Central Asia. The discussion will span Armenia,

coverage, and Uzbekistan, bringing together macro, sovereign
and corporate credit perspectives. Sign up here

Dmitry Dolgin, James Wilson and Egor Fedorov
The Caucasus and Central Asia have emerged from recent global
shocks with stronger sovereign balance sheets, improving credit
ratings and growing international investor interest. But can that
resilience continue into 2027, and how is the investment landscape
changing as the region becomes more accessible to global capital?
Join ING’s Dmitry Dolgin, Chief Economist, CCA and Russia, James
Wilson, Sovereign Debt Strategist, and Egor Fedorov, Senior Credit
Analyst, as they discuss the macro, sovereign debt and credit
outlook for the region. Sign up here

## Document 482: 【國泰證期研究部】AI PC產業-AI PC需求強勁，高階機種挹注營運-20261117

Source PDF: all report pdf/【國泰證期研究部】AI PC產業-AI PC需求強勁，高階機種挹注營運-20261117.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1 
產業更新 – AI PC 產業 
AI PC 需求強勁，高階機種挹注營運 
What’s New – RTX Spark 高階筆電需求強勁 
NVIDIA RTX Spark正式開賣，華碩、Dell、HP、聯想、Microsoft 與微星等六家品牌共推出
九款筆電，美國起售價 US$2,599.99起，台灣 128GB 版本售價逾 NT$23 萬。RTX Spark 最
高搭載 128GB Unified Memory，可於本地端執行 1,200 億參數 LLM。儘管定價遠高於一般
高階筆電，華碩首波 24GB 至 128GB 各規格 產品到貨即售罄，微星亦向 NVIDIA 追加晶片配
額，主要買家為企業、AI 開發者與內容創作者。企業目標由本地設備處理約 80%的基礎工作
以降低 Token 成本，顯示高算力、大容量 Unified Memory 的本地 Agent 平台已有明確客群。 
Trends/Outlook – 觸控 MacBook Pro 推升高階 ASP 
Apple 近期推出首款觸控螢幕 MacBook Pro，採 Tandem OLED 的 PROLED XDR 面板，14 
吋／16 吋起售價 US$2,899／3,399，較同晶片 mini-LED 機型高 US$400；M6 MacBook 
Pro 則維持 US$1,999 起，較 2025 年 M5 上市價高出 25.0%。M6 的 170GB/s 頻寬僅限 
24GB 以上版本，顯示 Apple 將 Local AI 效能集中於高容量配置，引導升級記憶體，顯示
Apple以新增高價位產品線推升產品組合。綜觀整體市場，3Q26 全球 PC 出貨 YoY -20.1%
（IDC 初估），記憶體合約價漲幅雖收斂但仍持續上揚，Intel、AMD 與華碩皆預期 2027 年 
PC 出貨持續衰退。研究部預期記憶體價格將居高不下，可能要到 4Q27 才會見到下滑，市場
「總量偏弱、高階 AI PC 強」的分化現象將持續。 
Investment Summary 
1. RTX Spark 與觸控 MacBook Pro 上市，推升高階筆電定價區間，兩者均鎖定價格敏感
度低的企業、AI 開發者與內容創作者。隨本地端執行 AI Agent 可降低雲端 Token 支出，
大容量 Unified Memory 成為高階機種主要規格，品牌廠成長動能將由出貨量轉向 ASP、
單機 Memory Content 與產品組合，3Q26 華碩廣義 AI PC 營收 YoY 逾 +100%、Intel 
AI PC 營收 YoY +35%，已反映此一趨勢。AMD 新平台亦支援最高 192GB Unified 
Memory，競爭焦點已由 NPU 轉向整機架構。 
2. 華碩 3Q26 PC 出貨年減 8.6%，PC 營收仍年增逾 25%，顯示產品組合升級可有效抵銷
出貨下滑。具備 RTX Spark 首波產品、高階機種占比高及 AI 伺服器業務的品牌廠，營
運仍具韌性。看好華碩(2357 TT)，其 ProArt P14／P16 為 RTX Spark 機種占比擴大，
加上伺服器營收強勁貢獻，營運維持成長；另正向看待微星(2377 TT)等品牌廠，其已將 
RTX Spark 與高階機種導入筆電、桌機與工作站，有利整機 ASP 與產品組合持續優化。

產業報告

2 
RTX Spark 高階筆電需求強勁 
NVIDIA RTX Spark 筆電近期正式開賣，華碩(2357 TT)、Dell(DELL US)、HP(HPQ US)、聯想
(0992 HK)、Microsoft(MSFT US)與微星(2377 TT)等六家品牌皆分別推出相關機種，為 NVIDIA首
度以自家 Grace CPU搭配 Blackwell RTX GPU的完整平台導入 Windows 11筆電。RTX Spark 
N1X 分為兩種配置：高階版本整合 20 核心 Grace CPU、6,144 核心 Blackwell RTX GPU 及
24~128GB Unified Memory，適用筆電與小型桌機；入門版本則為 18 核心 CPU、5,120 核心
GPU及 24GB／32GB Unified Memory，對應各品牌 US$2,599.99起之入門機種。高階版本最高
具備 1 Petaflop FP4算力，可於本地端執行 1,200億參數 LLM。 
表一：RTX Spark 首波主要筆電機種美國起售價 
品牌 機種 起售價 
Dell XPS 16 US$3,799.99 
微星 Prestige N16 Flip AI+ US$3,299.99 
HP OmniBook Ultra 16 US$3,199.99 
聯想 Yoga 9n US$3,099.99 
HP OmniBook X 14 US$2,999.99 
華碩 ProArt P16 US$2,799.99 
華碩 ProArt P14 US$2,599.99 
Microsoft Surface Laptop Ultra US$2,599.99 
資料來源：國泰證期研究部整理 
台灣市場方面，華碩 ProArt P14／P16 128GB版本售價分別為 NT$25萬／27萬，64GB版本自
NT$17萬／18萬起；微星 Prestige N16 Flip AI+ 128GB版本售價 NT$24萬、64GB版本 NT$14
萬。高階主力機種售價皆逾 NT$20萬，市場原先對首波需求偏向保守。 
然而實際需求卻優於預期。華碩首波 24GB 至 128GB 各規格產品到貨後即迅速售罄，全球需求強
勁，短期出現供應瓶頸；微星首批筆電開放預購後即售罄，已向 NVIDIA 爭取更多晶片配額。
NVIDIA也表示，產品價格雖高但需求強勁，並點名開發 AI服務與應用的企業為重要需求來源。從
客群來看，首波買家主要為內容創作者及有地端 AI 開發需求的企業。顯示企業正採取「地端先運
算、雲端再精修」的 Hybrid 架構，由本地設備處理約 80~90%的基礎工作，再將需進一步處理的
內容送往雲端，以降低 Token 成本並兼顧資料隱私。首波訂單主要來自企業與 AI 開發者等高價值
客群，對價格敏感度低且具明確的本地 AI需求，有助支撐品牌廠高階產品組合與毛利率。隨 11月
第二批產品出貨、64GB版本放量，以及 1Q27中低階 N1版本推出，RTX Spark可望由專業用戶
逐步擴大至高階主流市場。軟體生態亦同步到位，Adobe已針對 Photoshop、Premiere等軟體進
行最佳化，隨 Windows on Arm相容性持續改善，將進一步擴大消費客群。

產業報告

2026/11/17

3 
蘋果推出觸控 MacBook Pro，以新增高階產品線拉高 ASP 
蘋果(AAPL US)上月發表首款觸控螢幕 Mac，搭載 Tandem OLED 面板(PROLED XDR)、採 M5 
Pro／M5 Max晶片的 MacBook Pro，14吋與 16吋起售價分別為 US$2,899與 US$3,399。觸控
機型並未取代既有產品，而是定位於同晶片 mini-LED 機型之上，起始配置相同下售價高出
US$400。蘋果也推出 14吋 M6 MacBook Pro，採 2奈米製程的 M6晶片，Neural Engine由單
組 16核心升級為 Dual 16-core，記憶體頻寬由 153GB/s提升至 170GB/s。 
圖一、圖二：蘋果 MacBook Pro with PROLED XDR 
資料來源：Apple、國泰證期研究部整理 
M6 MacBook Pro起售價 US$1,999，雖與調漲後的 M5機型相同，但較 M5上市價 US$1,599高
出 25.0%，且起始配置仍為 16GB。170GB/s頻寬僅限 24GB與 32GB版本，蘋果主要效能宣稱亦
以 32GB版本測得，顯示蘋果在記憶體成本高漲下，選擇保留基本款規格，將 Local AI效能差異集
中於高容量配置，引導使用者升級記憶體。搭配觸控 OLED 新產品線，蘋果的定價策略由「既有產
品漲價」轉向「新增更高價位產品」，有利整體 ASP提升。

PC 總量下滑，高階 AI PC 營收逆勢成長 
3Q26全球 PC市場未見傳統旺季效應。單季出貨 6,205萬台(-9.0% QoQ，-19.9% YoY)，連續兩
季年減，主要原因在於 1H26 品牌廠與通路提前備貨、終端售價高檔及零組件供給受限三者疊加。
然而高階與 AI機種表現明顯優於市場平均。華碩 3Q26 PC出貨 550萬台(-8.6% YoY)，PC營收年
增逾 25%，其中內含 NPU的 AI PC營收年增逾 100%，占 PC營收 53%；電競、高階、AI PC與
商用 PC 等高價值產品合計占比 65%。華碩 4Q26 指引伺服器營收季增 150%，PC 營收則預期季
減 15%。蘋果 3Q26 PC 出貨年減 11.3%，同期 Mac 營收卻成長 23.7%。Intel CCPG(Client 
Computing and Physical AI Group)營收 US$8.1bn(-5% YoY)，AI PC營收卻年增 35%。 
綜上所述，出貨量與營收的落差主要來自 ASP 上升與產品組合轉向高階。RTX Spark、AMD 
Ryzen AI Max PRO 400系列(最高 192GB)及蘋果 M系列皆以大容量 Unified Memory支援本地
大型模型，AI PC競爭焦點已由 NPU TOPS轉向 CPU、GPU與記憶體的整機架構。

投資 顧問 
產業報告

2026/11/17

4 
記憶體價格居高不下，結構性分化將持續 
記憶體合約價漲幅雖收斂，但尚未出現反轉訊號。供給面方面，原廠持續將先進製程產能投入
Server應用，27年 PC DRAM供給可能進一步縮減；PC OEM考量 27年供給吃緊，4Q26仍維持
積極採購。成本壓力已反映於各廠對 27年 PC市場的展望。預估 26年產業 PC出貨約年減 12%、
27 年約年減 10%，並指引 4Q26 CCPG 營收季減逾 20%；華碩預估 27 年全球 PC 市場萎縮
10~15%，上半年需求偏弱、下半年才逐步回升。在 DRAM 與 CPU 價格維持高檔下，為壓低
BOM成本，品牌廠已調降主流機種 SSD容量，中低階機種面臨「降規漲價」壓力。 
綜合上述，研究部預期記憶體價格將居高不下，可能要到 4Q27 才會見到下滑，短期仍將對 PC 市
場造成壓力，尤以價格敏感的消費型與中低階機種影響最大。反觀 RTX Spark 與觸控 MacBook 
Pro等高階產品，主要客群為企業、AI開發者與內容創作者，對價格敏感度較低，且本地端執行 AI 
Agent可降低雲端 Token支出，需求較具韌性。整體而言，PC市場「總量偏弱、高階 AI PC較強」
的結構性分化將持續，產業動能將由出貨量擴張轉向 ASP、Memory Content 與產品組合升級，
具備高階 AI PC產品線與定價能力的品牌廠，可望持續擴大與同業的營運差距。
